As most Spectres will be Narrator characters, there will be very little call for Psycheguidmg in gamesof Oblivion. Below are the basic guidelines for Psyche Traits.
psyche Archefypes
A Spectre's Psyche is a somewhat idealized self. With that m mind, 1r takes on an idealized Archetype. Below are some samples.
Agent: Always looking out for your best interests, seeking to find something constructive anJ positive for you to Jo, this Psyche wants co manage you, guide you and generally help you better yourself.
Comrade: This Psyche is the shoulder to cry on, the friendly smile on a bad day, always ready to lend a hand or helpyou through a bad patch. Like any old friend, the Comrade knows you intimately, and accepts the bad along with the good.
Confessor: Stem but kindly, this Psyche's gruff exterior hides a fount of love and compassion. Although affronted by the sins it is witness to, the Confessor would nver think of revealing them co others.
Counselor: The Conselor is the person you can tell anyrhing, that part of you prepared to sit and listen co another person's problems without judging them or be­ rating them.The roleof the Counselor isto help the character realize where her own problems are, and assist with setting strategies to deal with them.

Nurturer: This Psyche is maternal, supportive and caring. It is concerned about the kind of people the host hangs around with, and considers them a bad influence. Although the Nurturer may scold, it never loses its temper.
Saint: Few Spectres are strong enough to stand up co this Psyche. Proud, commanding and authorirarive, the Saint stands out as an incorruptible beacon of purity in the host's mind. The Saint knows evil exists, bur has no time for it.
Savior: This Psyche L the way and the light, the road to salvation. Constantly preaching to the host, the Savior wants the character to listen only to it. No other can lead the host to redemption except the Savior. The Savior is self-sacrificing, prepared to suffer any harm should that m some way advance the redemption of the Spectre.
Composure
The Psyche is powered by a Trait called Composure. Composure represents the degree of serenity and peace the Psyche possesses. There are rwo kinds of Compo­ sure, Composure Traits and Temporary Composure pomts. Ir 1s from the latter that Composure points are spent, powering Fronds. Composure Traits represent the de­ gree of control the Psyche has over a character. The Psyche always begins with one Permanent Composure Trait.
Temporary Composure points begin at a rating equal to the Psyche's number of Permanent Composure Traits, but fluctuate thereafter. Temporary Composure points can and often do exceed the number of Composure Traits. Composure can be in­ creased by the use of certain Fronds. The Dark Arcanos Larceny is another sour e of Composure, and it can also be increaseJ by fulfilling Passions.
IfTemporary Composure ever reaches 10, the Psyche can trade all 10 points for one point of Permanent Composure, bringing the character that much closer to re­ demption.

In Spectre Cathar:;is, the Psyche rakes over for one scene or half an hour, whichever end sooner

Crossover ules
ven more so than ocher Mind's Eye Theatre games, Oblivion 1s a game that can be played in tandem with Masquerade and Apocalypse. The following crossover rules have been adapted from the crossover rules included m Wraith: The Oblivion 2nd Edition, with some modifications for greater playability
,111 ma live-action M!Ctmg. As wirhany Mind's Eye Theatresupple­ ment, this crossover section should be used as a guideline, not holy scripture. Storyrellers should always feel free to modify
rules to make chem work better for their particular group.
Prior experience shows rhat any time you have a significant number of wraiths m with ocher supernatural or mortal characters, it is a good idea ro bend the rules a little in the interest of player interaction. Having a particularly powerful Haunr in which wraith characters with Embody may fully manifest for litrle or no Pathos cost isa good way to increase interaction, though you may find that che focus of the game shife:; to that ,ine location, due to the fact that it's the only place mOl>t of the other characters can interact with wraiths. An even better idea is to make sure that every

wraith has a few of the other types of characters as Fetters they are attuned to. This
encourages wraiths to become involveJ with affairs in the Skinlands without r<'­ srricring them ro a given place, or completely removing the sense of isolation.
Crossoverswit Masquerade
Giovanni
These necromancers of Italian descent are among the greatest enemie the Re5t­ less have m the world of the Quick. While some among their number are scholars who wish only to i;tudy and comprehend death, the Giovanni are for the most part a collecuon of utterly nithl and power-hungry individuals, who seek nothing more than Jommance over the worlds of both the livrng and the dead. Tc) that end, rhey havegained power nver many of the financial centers of the Quick, and control much of the commerce in the living world. But mere temporal power is seen as onlya means to an end by these vampires. Their study of Necromancy has caught chemp greatdeal about how to summon anJ control wraiths.
Summon Spirit: As written in Laws of the Night, but 1( the "person or obJect in a room rhat had some connection ro the spirit m its life" is a Fetter, theNccromancer begins the Social Challenge one hid up, due to the particularly strong connection.
